    if(window.m17FromMeasureMenu2!==true)return false;
    /* Lock the exact operation selected in Menu 2 before leaving Menu 17.
       m2SketchMode is the fallback for entry paths that did not set m2RealMode. */
    var mode=window.m2RealMode||window.m2SketchMode||'point';
    if(mode!=='line'&&mode!=='area')mode='point';
    window.m2RealMode=mode;
    window.m2SketchMode=mode;
    /* Finalize the in-progress line/area before returning to Menu 2. */
    if((mode==='line'||mode==='area') && typeof window.m17FinishCurrentDrawing==='function'){
      var pts=(window.m17GetDrawPoints?window.m17GetDrawPoints():[]);
      var min=mode==='area'?3:2;
      if(pts.length>0 && pts.length<min){showToast(mode==='area'?'برای اتمام مساحت حداقل سه نقطه لازم است':'برای اتمام خط حداقل دو نقطه لازم است');return false;}
      if(pts.length>=min)window.m17FinishCurrentDrawing();
    }
    window.m17FromMeasureMenu2=false;
    showPage('realMeasure');
    /* Restore the matching schematic mode, redraw from the finished Menu 17
       snapshot, then bring the entire schematic card into view on Android. */
    setTimeout(function(){
      try{
        if(typeof window.m2ReturnToSchematic==='function')window.m2ReturnToSchematic(mode);
        else if(typeof window.m275Refresh==='function')window.m275Refresh();
      }catch(e){console.error('M2_RETURN_SCHEMATIC',e);}
    },120);
  }catch(e){console.error('M17_FINISH_MEASURE_ENTRY',e);showToast('بازگشت به منوی ۲ انجام نشد');}
  return false;
};

function openRealStakeMenu(){
  try{
    /* V274: keep the realStake hash in sync so the global BACK button knows where it is. */
    goPage('realStake');
    setTimeout(function(){
      try{
        if(typeof m17Init==='function')m17Init();
        var live=q('m17LiveLaunch');
        if(live && !live.dataset.realStakeBridge){
          live.dataset.realStakeBridge='1';
          live.addEventListener('click',function(){
            try{showPage('menu17');if(typeof m17Init==='function')m17Init();}catch(e){}
          },true);
        }
        var stakeBtn=document.getElementById('m17StakeBtn');if(stakeBtn)stakeBtn.click();
      }catch(e){console.error('openRealStakeMenu',e);}
    },40);
  }catch(e){console.error('openRealStakeMenu',e);}
  return false;
}

function realStakeStartLive(){
  try{
    var lat,lng;
    if(realStakeMethod==='utm'){
      var x=Number(String($('realStakeX')?$('realStakeX').value:'').replace(/,/g,'')),
          y=Number(String($('realStakeY')?$('realStakeY').value:'').replace(/,/g,'')),
          z=Number(String($('realStakeZone')?$('realStakeZone').value:'').replace(/,/g,''));
      if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y)||!isFinite(z)||z<1||z>60){showToast('X / Y / Zone را صحیح وارد کن');return false;}
      if(typeof window.m17ConvertUtmToWgs84!=='function'){showToast('ارتباط با نقشه منوی ۱۷ آماده نیست');return false;}
      var northern = y < 10000000;
      var conv = (typeof window.m17UtmToWgs84==='function')
        ? window.m17UtmToWgs84(x,y,z,northern)
        : window.m17ConvertUtmToWgs84(x,y,z);
      if(!conv||!isFinite(conv.lat)||!isFinite(conv.lng)){showToast('تبدیل مختصات هدف انجام نشد');return false;}
      lat=conv.lat;lng=conv.lng;
    }else{
      lng=Number(String($('realStakeLng')?$('realStakeLng').value:'').replace(/,/g,''));
      lat=Number(String($('realStakeLat')?$('realStakeLat').value:'').replace(/,/g,''));
      if(!isFinite(lat)||!isFinite(lng)||lat<-90||lat>90||lng<-180||lng>180){showToast('طول و عرض جغرافیایی را صحیح وارد کن');return false;}
    }
    showPage('menu17');
    setTimeout(function(){
      try{
        if(typeof m17Init==='function')m17Init();
        if(typeof m17LiveOpen!=='function'){showToast('بخش پیاده‌سازی زنده آماده نیست');return;}
        m17LiveOpen({lat:lat,lng:lng},'P'+((typeof window.m17GetStakeCount==='function'?window.m17GetStakeCount():0)+1));
      }catch(e){console.error('realStakeStartLive',e);showToast('ورود به پیاده‌سازی زنده انجام نشد');}
    },180);
  }catch(e){console.error('realStakeStartLive',e);showToast('پیاده‌سازی زنده اجرا نشد');}
  return false;
}



/* V11: Windows mouse-click bridge.  Menu navigation is handled here in capture phase so desktop clicks do not depend on inline onclick/hash behavior. */
(function(){
  function menuTarget(node){
    while(node && node!==document.body){
      if(node.id==='jobNavBtn' || (node.classList && node.classList.contains('menu-btn'))) return node;
      node=node.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',function(ev){
    if(ev.button!==undefined && ev.button!==0) return;
    var el=menuTarget(ev.target);
    if(!el || !document.documentElement.classList.contains('desktop-windows')) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    ev.stopImmediatePropagation();
    if(el.id==='jobNavBtn'){goPage('job');return;}
    if(el.id==='leicaSimulatorNavBtn'){goPage('leica');return;}
    if(el.id==='measureMenu'){goPage('realMeasure');return;}
    if(el.id==='stakeMenu'){goPage('realStake');return;}
    var href=el.getAttribute('href')||'',target=href.charAt(0)==='#'?href.slice(1):'';
    if(target) goPage(target);
  },true);
})();
window.showPage=showPage;window.loadMenu1StationPoint=loadMenu1StationPoint;window.loadMenu1OrientationPoint=loadMenu1OrientationPoint;window.changeMeasureAngleMode=changeMeasureAngleMode;window.setLeicaLineFromJob=setLeicaLineFromJob;window.setLeicaLineManual=setLeicaLineManual;window.clearReferenceLine=clearReferenceLine;

/* V379 — explicit JOB create/rename controls.  No prompt, no overloaded saveJob. */
function createNewJobFromInput(){
  try{
    var input=$("jobNewName"), n=input?String(input.value||'').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,''):'';
    if(!n){showToast('نام JOB جدید را وارد کن');if(input)input.focus();return false;}
    var current=(job&&job.name)?String(job.name).trim():'';
    if(current && n.toLowerCase()===current.toLowerCase()){showToast('نام JOB جدید باید متفاوت باشد');return false;}
    try{if(window.miladJobArchiveCurrent362)window.miladJobArchiveCurrent362();}catch(e){}
    var st={job:{name:n,created:new Date().toISOString()},setup:{stationSet:false,orientationSet:false,stationName:'',sx:null,sy:null,sz:null,stationHI:null,stationDP:'',orientationMethod:'azimuth',azimuth:null,orientationDistance:null,backName:'',bx:null,by:null,bz:null},points:[]};
    if(window.miladGeoApplyJobState)window.miladGeoApplyJobState(st);
    localStorage.setItem('miladgeo_active_job_v362',n);
    if(input)input.value='';
    var rn=$("jobName");if(rn)rn.value='';
    updateAllUI();var h=$("jobCurrentNameHint");if(h)h.textContent=n;try{updateJobOutput363();}catch(e){}
    /* Register the new empty JOB immediately so its quick-recall card appears. */
    setTimeout(function(){try{if(window.miladJobAutoSync362)window.miladJobAutoSync362();}catch(e){}try{if(window.miladJobRefreshQuickCards390)window.miladJobRefreshQuickCards390();}catch(e){}},180);
    showToast('JOB جدید ایجاد شد ✓');
    return false;
  }catch(e){console.error('V379 createNewJob',e);showToast('ایجاد JOB جدید انجام نشد');return false;}
}
function renameJobFromInput(){
  try{
    var old=(job&&job.name)?String(job.name).trim():'';
    if(!old){showToast('ابتدا یک JOB ایجاد کن');return false;}
    var input=$("jobName"), n=input?String(input.value||'').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,''):'';
    if(!n){showToast('نام جدید را وارد کن');if(input)input.focus();return false;}
    if(n.toLowerCase()===old.toLowerCase()){showToast('نام JOB تغییر نکرد');return false;}
    /* Remove the old archive first; then apply the same live state under the new name. */
    try{
      var key='miladgeo_jobs_v362',a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');
      a=a.filter(function(j){return String(j.name||'').toLowerCase()!==old.toLowerCase();});
      localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(a));
    }catch(e){}
    var st=window.miladGeoGetJobState?window.miladGeoGetJobState():{job:job,setup:setup,points:points};
    st=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st||{}));st.job=st.job||{};st.job.name=n;
    if(window.miladGeoApplyJobState)window.miladGeoApplyJobState(st);
    localStorage.setItem('miladgeo_active_job_v362',n);
    input.value=n;
    updateAllUI();var h=$("jobCurrentNameHint");if(h)h.textContent=n;try{updateJobOutput363();}catch(e){}
    showToast('نام JOB تغییر کرد ✓');
    return false;
  }catch(e){console.error('V379 renameJob',e);showToast('تغییر نام JOB انجام نشد');return false;}
}

function menu1PointLabel(p){return String((p&&p.no)||'P'+String((p&&p.id)||''));}
function refreshMenu1ReferenceSelects(){
  var a=$("menu1RefA"),b=$("menu1RefB");if(!a||!b)return;
  var oldA=a.value,oldB=b.value,opts='<option value="">— انتخاب نقطه —</option>';
  var seen={};
  (Array.isArray(points)?points:[]).forEach(function(p){
    if(!p||!finite(Number(p.x))||!finite(Number(p.y)))return;
    var key=String(p.id);if(seen[key])return;seen[key]=1;
    opts+='<option value="'+escapeHTML(key)+'">'+escapeHTML(menu1PointLabel(p))+' — X '+fmt(p.x,2)+' | Y '+fmt(p.y,2)+'</option>';
  });
  a.innerHTML=opts;b.innerHTML=opts;
  if(oldA&&a.querySelector('option[value="'+CSS.escape(oldA)+'"]'))a.value=oldA;
  if(oldB&&b.querySelector('option[value="'+CSS.escape(oldB)+'"]'))b.value=oldB;
  if(setup.referenceLineSet){a.value=String(setup.referenceAId||'');b.value=String(setup.referenceBId||'');}
}
function findMenu1Point(id){return (Array.isArray(points)?points:[]).find(function(p){return String(p.id)===String(id);})||null;}
function menu1GridAzimuth(de,dn){var a=Math.atan2(Number(de),Number(dn))*180/Math.PI;if(a<0)a+=360;return a;}
function renderReferenceLineUI(){
  var card=$("menu1ReferenceCard"),state=$("menu1ReferenceState"),res=$("menu1ReferenceResult"),active=$("menu1ReferenceActive"),badge=$("menu1SetupBadge"),cur=$("menu1CurrentSummary");
  var station=!!setup.stationSet,orient=!!setup.orientationSet,ref=!!setup.referenceLineSet;
  [[card,ref],[$("menu1StationCard"),station],[$("menu1OrientationCard"),orient]].forEach(function(x){if(!x[0])return;x[0].classList.toggle('ok',x[1]);x[0].classList.toggle('off',!x[1]);});
  if(state)state.textContent=ref?'ACTIVE':'NOT SET';
  if(badge){var complete=station&&orient;badge.textContent=complete?(ref?'SETUP + REFERENCE READY':'SETUP READY — REFERENCE OPTIONAL'):'SETUP INCOMPLETE';badge.style.background=complete?'#dcefd8':'#eef3eb';}
  if(cur){cur.style.display='none';}
  if(res){if(ref)res.innerHTML='<div class="menu1-ref-result"><div><small>ΔE</small><b>'+fmt(setup.referenceDE,3)+' m</b></div><div><small>ΔN</small><b>'+fmt(setup.referenceDN,3)+' m</b></div><div><small>LENGTH</small><b>'+fmt(setup.referenceLength,3)+' m</b></div><div><small>AZIMUTH</small><b>'+fmt(setup.referenceAzimuth,4)+'°</b></div></div>';else res.innerHTML='';}
  if(active)active.innerHTML=ref?'<div class="menu1-ref-active">✓ خط مرجع فعال است: '+escapeHTML(setup.referenceAName||'A')+' → '+escapeHTML(setup.referenceBName||'B')+'</div>':'';
  refreshMenu1ReferenceSelects();
}
function setReferenceLine(){
  var aId=$("menu1RefA")?$("menu1RefA").value:'',bId=$("menu1RefB")?$("menu1RefB").value:'';
  if(!aId||!bId){showToast('ابتدا نقطه A و B را انتخاب کن');return false;}
  if(String(aId)===String(bId)){showToast('نقطه A و B نباید یکسان باشند');return false;}
  var a=findMenu1Point(aId),b=findMenu1Point(bId);if(!a||!b){showToast('نقاط خط مرجع پیدا نشدند');return false;}
  var de=Number(b.x)-Number(a.x),dn=Number(b.y)-Number(a.y),len=Math.hypot(de,dn);if(!finite(len)||len<=0){showToast('طول خط مرجع معتبر نیست');return false;}
  setup.referenceLineSet=true;setup.referenceAId=String(a.id);setup.referenceBId=String(b.id);setup.referenceAName=menu1PointLabel(a);setup.referenceBName=menu1PointLabel(b);setup.referenceAX=Number(a.x);setup.referenceAY=Number(a.y);setup.referenceBX=Number(b.x);setup.referenceBY=Number(b.y);setup.referenceDE=de;setup.referenceDN=dn;setup.referenceLength=len;setup.referenceAzimuth=menu1GridAzimuth(de,dn);saveJSON(SETUP_KEY,setup);renderReferenceLineUI();if(typeof renderSurveyMap==='function')renderSurveyMap();showToast('REFERENCE LINE فعال شد ✓');return false;
}
function clearReferenceLine(){if(!setup.referenceLineSet){showToast('خط مرجعی فعال نیست');return false;}delete setup.referenceLineSet;delete setup.referenceAId;delete setup.referenceBId;delete setup.referenceAName;delete setup.referenceBName;delete setup.referenceAX;delete setup.referenceAY;delete setup.referenceBX;delete setup.referenceBY;delete setup.referenceDE;delete setup.referenceDN;delete setup.referenceLength;delete setup.referenceAzimuth;delete setup.referenceSource;saveJSON(SETUP_KEY,setup);renderReferenceLineUI();if(typeof renderSurveyMap==='function')renderSurveyMap();showToast('خط مرجع حذف شد');return false;}

function normalizeAzimuth(a){a=a%360;if(a<0)a+=360;return a;}
function calculateAzimuth(x1,y1,x2,y2){return normalizeAzimuth(Math.atan2(x2-x1,y2-y1)*180/Math.PI);}
function setOrientation(){if(!setup.stationSet){showToast('Set Station first');return false;}var m=$("orientMethod").value,a,d,p=null;if(m==='jobPoint'){var pid=$("orientationPointSelect")?$("orientationPointSelect").value:'';p=menu1FindPointById(pid);if(!p){showToast('نقطه پشت‌دید را از JOB انتخاب کن');return false;}if(String(p.id)===String(setup.stationPointId)){showToast('Backsight cannot equal Station');return false;}a=calculateAzimuth(setup.sx,setup.sy,p.x,p.y);d=Math.hypot(Number(p.x)-Number(setup.sx),Number(p.y)-Number(setup.sy));setup.bx=Number(p.x);setup.by=Number(p.y);setup.bz=Number(p.z);setup.backName=p.no||menu1PointLabel(p);setup.orientationPointId=String(p.id);setup.orientationSource='job';}else if(m==='azimuth'){a=num($("manualAz").value);d=num($("orientationDistance").value)||0;if(!finite(a)||a<0||a>=360){showToast('Enter Azimuth 0–360');return false;}delete setup.orientationPointId;setup.orientationSource='manual';}else{var x=num($("bx").value),y=num($("by").value),z=num($("bz").value);if(!finite(x)||!finite(y)||!finite(z)){showToast('Enter Backsight X / Y / Z');return false;}d=Math.hypot(x-setup.sx,y-setup.sy);a=calculateAzimuth(setup.sx,setup.sy,x,y);setup.bx=x;setup.by=y;setup.bz=z;setup.backName=($("backName").value||'BS01').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'');delete setup.orientationPointId;setup.orientationSource='manual';}setup.orientationMethod=m;setup.azimuth=normalizeAzimuth(a);setup.orientationDistance=d;setup.orientationSet=true;saveJSON(SETUP_KEY,setup);$("orientationResult").innerHTML='<div class="result"><div class="az">'+fmt(setup.azimuth,4)+'°</div><div class="result-line"><span>Backsight</span><b>'+escapeHTML(setup.backName||'—')+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Length</span><b>'+fmt(d,3)+' m</b></div></div>';renderOrientationSet();updateAllUI();showToast('ORIENTATION SET ✓');return false;}
function setupComplete(){return setup.stationSet===true&&setup.orientationSet===true;}
function openMeasure(){if(!setupComplete()){showToast('Station & Orientation required');return false;}prepareNewPoint();showPage('measure');return false;}
function openStake(){if(!setupComplete()){showToast('Station & Orientation required');return false;}showPage('stake');setTimeout(function(){var z=q('stake3Zone');if(z&&!z.value){var u=wgs84ToUTM(state.lat,state.lng);z.value=String(u.zone);}},20);return false;}
var areaPointCount=3,areaLastPoints=null,areaLastStats=null;
function areaRowHTML(i){return '<div class="area-point" data-area-row="'+i+'"><div class="pt-label">P'+i+'</div><div><label>X / Easting</label><input id="areaX'+i+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="X"></div><div><label>Y / Northing</label><input id="areaY'+i+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="Y"></div><button type="button" class="area-remove" onclick="removeAreaPoint('+i+');return false;">×</button></div>';}
function renderAreaPoints(){var box=$("areaPoints");if(!box)return;var old=[];for(var k=1;k<=areaPointCount;k++){old.push({x:$("areaX"+k)?$("areaX"+k).value:'',y:$("areaY"+k)?$("areaY"+k).value:''});}var out='';for(var i=1;i<=areaPointCount;i++)out+=areaRowHTML(i);box.innerHTML=out;for(i=1;i<=areaPointCount;i++){if($("areaX"+i))$("areaX"+i).value=old[i-1]?old[i-1].x:'';if($("areaY"+i))$("areaY"+i).value=old[i-1]?old[i-1].y:'';}updateAreaCoordinateSummary();}
function addAreaPoint(){areaPointCount++;renderAreaPoints();var el=$("areaX"+areaPointCount);if(el)el.focus();return false;}
function removeAreaPoint(i){if(areaPointCount<=3){showToast('حداقل ۳ نقطه لازم است');return false;}var vals=[];for(var j=1;j<=areaPointCount;j++)if(j!==i)vals.push({x:$("areaX"+j).value,y:$("areaY"+j).value});areaPointCount--;renderAreaPoints();for(j=1;j<=areaPointCount;j++){ $("areaX"+j).value=vals[j-1].x;$("areaY"+j).value=vals[j-1].y;}updateAreaCoordinateSummary();return false;}
function getAreaPoints(){var a=[];for(var i=1;i<=areaPointCount;i++){var x=num($("areaX"+i).value),y=num($("areaY"+i).value);if(!finite(x)||!finite(y)){showToast('مختصات P'+i+' کامل نیست');return null;}a.push({no:i,x:x,y:y});}if(a.length>=4){var f=a[0],l=a[a.length-1];if(Math.hypot(f.x-l.x,f.y-l.y)<1e-8){a.pop();}}if(a.length<3){showToast('حداقل ۳ نقطه یکتا لازم است');return null;}return a;}
function updateAreaCoordinateSummary(){drawAreaSketch();}
function areaSetSource(mode){document.querySelectorAll('.area-source-btn').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active')});var map={manual:0,job:1,excel:2};var bs=document.querySelectorAll('.area-source-btn');if(bs[map[mode]])bs[map[mode]].classList.add('active');if($("areaJobPicker"))$("areaJobPicker").style.display=mode==='job'?'block':'none';if($("areaExcelPicker"))$("areaExcelPicker").style.display=mode==='excel'?'block':'none';if(mode==='job')refreshAreaJobPoints();}
function refreshAreaJobPoints(){var s=$("areaJobPointSelect");if(!s)return;var out='<option value="">انتخاب نقطه...</option>';(Array.isArray(points)?points:[]).forEach(function(p){if(p&&finite(Number(p.x))&&finite(Number(p.y)))out+='<option value="'+escapeHTML(String(p.id||p.no))+'">'+escapeHTML(String(p.no||p.id||'P'))+' — X '+fmt(p.x,2)+' | Y '+fmt(p.y,2)+'</option>';});s.innerHTML=out;}
function areaAddJobPoint(){var s=$("areaJobPointSelect"),id=s?s.value:'';var p=(points||[]).find(function(q){return String(q.id||q.no)===String(id)});if(!p){showToast('نقطه را انتخاب کن');return false;}areaPointCount++;renderAreaPoints();$("areaX"+areaPointCount).value=p.x;$("areaY"+areaPointCount).value=p.y;showToast('نقطه به پلیگون اضافه شد ✓');return false;}
function areaImportFile(input){var f=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];if(!f)return false;var reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=function(ev){try{var text=String(ev.target.result||'');var rows=[];if(/\.(xlsx|xls)$/i.test(f.name)&&window.XLSX){var wb=XLSX.read(ev.target.result,{type:'array'}),ws=wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];rows=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws,{header:1,defval:''});}else rows=text.split(/\r?\n/).map(function(x){return x.split(/[;,\t]/)});var heads=(rows[0]||[]).map(function(x){return String(x).trim().toLowerCase()});var xi=heads.findIndex(function(x){return /^(x|e|easting|east|x\s*coordinate)$/.test(x)}),yi=heads.findIndex(function(x){return /^(y|n|northing|north|y\s*coordinate)$/.test(x)});if(xi<0)xi=0;if(yi<0)yi=1;var vals=[];for(var i=1;i<rows.length;i++){var x=areaExcelNum(rows[i][xi]),y=areaExcelNum(rows[i][yi]);if(isFinite(x)&&isFinite(y))vals.push({x:x,y:y});}if(vals.length<3){showToast('حداقل ۳ ردیف مختصات معتبر لازم است');return;}areaPointCount=Math.max(3,vals.length);renderAreaPoints();vals.forEach(function(v,k){$("areaX"+(k+1)).value=v.x;$("areaY"+(k+1)).value=v.y;});showToast(vals.length+' نقطه وارد شد ✓');}catch(e){showToast('خواندن فایل انجام نشد');console.error(e);}};if(/\.(xlsx|xls)$/i.test(f.name))reader.readAsArrayBuffer(f);else reader.readAsText(f);return false;}
function areaExcelNum(v){if(v===null||v===undefined||v==='')return NaN;var t=String(v).trim().replace(/[٬،]/g,',').replace(/\s/g,'');if(t.indexOf(',')>=0&&t.indexOf('.')<0)t=t.replace(',','.');return parseFloat(t);}
function areaSegments(a){var out=[];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var j=(i+1)%a.length,dx=a[j].x-a[i].x,dy=a[j].y-a[i].y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy),az=normalizeAzimuth(Math.atan2(dx,dy)*180/Math.PI);out.push({from:a[i].no,to:a[j].no,length:len,azimuth:az});}return out;}
function areaOrientation(a){var s=0;for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var j=(i+1)%a.length;s+=a[i].x*a[j].y-a[j].x*a[i].y;}return s>=0?'پادساعتگرد':'ساعتگرد';}
function areaCross(a,b,c){return (b.x-a.x)*(c.y-a.y)-(b.y-a.y)*(c.x-a.x);}
function areaOnSeg(a,b,p){return Math.min(a.x,b.x)-1e-9<=p.x&&p.x<=Math.max(a.x,b.x)+1e-9&&Math.min(a.y,b.y)-1e-9<=p.y&&p.y<=Math.max(a.y,b.y)+1e-9;}
function areaIntersect(a,b,c,d){var c1=areaCross(a,b,c),c2=areaCross(a,b,d),c3=areaCross(c,d,a),c4=areaCross(c,d,b);if(c1===0&&areaOnSeg(a,b,c))return true;if(c2===0&&areaOnSeg(a,b,d))return true;if(c3===0&&areaOnSeg(c,d,a))return true;if(c4===0&&areaOnSeg(c,d,b))return true;return ((c1>0)!=(c2>0))&&((c3>0)!=(c4>0));}
function areaValidate(a){var issues=[];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)for(var j=i+1;j<a.length;j++)if(Math.hypot(a[i].x-a[j].x,a[i].y-a[j].y)<1e-8)issues.push('نقطه تکراری: P'+a[i].no+' و P'+a[j].no);for(i=0;i<a.length;i++){var ni=(i+1)%a.length;for(j=i+1;j<a.length;j++){var nj=(j+1)%a.length;if(i===j||ni===j||nj===i)continue;if(areaIntersect(a[i],a[ni],a[j],a[nj]))issues.push('تقاطع اضلاع بین P'+a[i].no+'–P'+a[ni].no+' و P'+a[j].no+'–P'+a[nj].no);}}return issues;}
function areaCentroid(a,areaSigned){var A=areaSigned*2,cx=0,cy=0;for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var j=(i+1)%a.length,c=a[i].x*a[j].y-a[j].x*a[i].y;cx+=(a[i].x+a[j].x)*c;cy+=(a[i].y+a[j].y)*c;}return {x:cx/(3*A),y:cy/(3*A)};}
function calculateArea(){var a=getAreaPoints();if(!a)return false;var sum=0;for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var j=(i+1)%a.length;sum+=a[i].x*a[j].y-a[j].x*a[i].y;}var area=Math.abs(sum)/2,per=areaSegments(a).reduce(function(t,s){return t+s.length},0),issues=areaValidate(a),cent=areaCentroid(a,sum);areaLastPoints=a;areaLastStats={area:area,per:per,centroid:cent,segments:areaSegments(a),orientation:areaOrientation(a)};var v=$("areaValidation");v.className='area-validation '+(issues.length?'bad':'ok');v.innerHTML=issues.length?'⚠ '+issues.join('<br>'):'✓ پلیگون معتبر است — '+a.length+' رأس — جهت: '+areaLastStats.orientation;$("areaResult").innerHTML='<div class="area-result-main">'+fmt(area,2)+' <small>m²</small></div><div class="area-stat-grid"><div class="area-stat"><small>محیط</small><b>'+fmt(per,3)+' m</b></div><div class="area-stat"><small>تعداد رئوس</small><b>'+a.length+'</b></div><div class="area-stat"><small>مرکز X</small><b>'+fmt(cent.x,3)+'</b></div><div class="area-stat"><small>مرکز Y</small><b>'+fmt(cent.y,3)+'</b></div></div>';$("areaUnitSummary").innerHTML='<div class="area-unit-item">هکتار<b>'+fmt(area/10000,4)+'</b></div><div class="area-unit-item">کیلومترمربع<b>'+fmt(area/1000000,6)+'</b></div><div class="area-unit-item">acre<b>'+fmt(area*0.000247105381,4)+'</b></div><div class="area-unit-item">فوت مربع<b>'+fmt(area*10.7639104167,2)+'</b></div>';areaRenderSides();drawAreaSketch();showToast('محاسبه مساحت انجام شد ✓');return false;}
function areaRenderSides(){var box=$("areaSides"),a=areaLastPoints;if(!box||!a||!areaLastStats)return;var h='<table class="area-table"><thead><tr><th>ضلع</th><th>از</th><th>به</th><th>طول (m)</th><th>آزیموت (°)</th></tr></thead><tbody>';areaLastStats.segments.forEach(function(s,i){h+='<tr><td>'+(i+1)+'</td><td>P'+s.from+'</td><td>P'+s.to+'</td><td>'+fmt(s.length,3)+'</td><td>'+fmt(s.azimuth,4)+'</td></tr>';});box.innerHTML=h+'</tbody></table>'}
function drawAreaSketch(){
 var svg=$("areaSketch");if(!svg)return;
 var a=areaLastPoints||[], off=areaOffsetLast||null, splits=areaSplitLast||[];
 if(!a.length){svg.innerHTML='<text x="350" y="180" text-anchor="middle" fill="#6f786f" font-size="16">مختصات پلیگون اینجا نمایش داده می‌شود</text>';return;}
 var all=a.slice();if(off)all=all.concat(off);splits.forEach(function(q){all=all.concat(q||[]);});
 var minX=Math.min.apply(null,all.map(function(p){return p.x})),maxX=Math.max.apply(null,all.map(function(p){return p.x})),minY=Math.min.apply(null,all.map(function(p){return p.y})),maxY=Math.max.apply(null,all.map(function(p){return p.y}));
 var pad=70,w=700,h=330,rx=maxX-minX||1,ry=maxY-minY||1,scale=Math.min((w-2*pad)/rx,(h-2*pad)/ry);if(!isFinite(scale)||scale<=0)scale=1;
 var ox=(w-(minX+maxX)*scale)/2,oy=(h+(minY+maxY)*scale)/2;
 function sx(x){return ox+x*scale}function sy(y){return oy-y*scale}
 function pts(p){return p.map(function(q){return sx(q.x)+','+sy(q.y)}).join(' ')}
 var out='';
 out+='<rect x="0" y="0" width="700" height="330" rx="10" fill="#f7faf6"/>';
 out+='<line x1="48" y1="292" x2="48" y2="48" stroke="#637065" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M48 38 l-7 13 h14 z" fill="#315b34"/><text x="48" y="28" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" font-weight="900" fill="#315b34">N</text>';
 if(a.length>=3)out+='<polygon points="'+pts(a)+'" fill="#b9d8b7" fill-opacity="0.48" stroke="#2f6435" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>';
 if(off&&off.length>=3)out+='<polygon points="'+pts(off)+'" fill="#dbead8" fill-opacity="0.18" stroke="#4d7b50" stroke-width="3" stroke-dasharray="9 6" stroke-linejoin="round"/>';
 splits.forEach(function(q){if(q&&q.length>=3)out+='<polygon points="'+pts(q)+'" fill="#d8e9d5" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#668a68" stroke-width="2" stroke-dasharray="6 5" stroke-linejoin="round"/>';});
 // Original points and labels
 for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){
   var j=(i+1)%a.length,px=sx(a[i].x),py=sy(a[i].y),qx=sx(a[j].x),qy=sy(a[j].y),mx=(px+qx)/2,my=(py+qy)/2;
   out+='<circle cx="'+px+'" cy="'+py+'" r="6" fill="#245d2c" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2"/>';
   out+='<text x="'+(px+10)+'" y="'+(py-10)+'" font-size="12" font-weight="900" fill="#18351c" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="3" paint-order="stroke">P'+a[i].no+'</text>';
   if(a.length>1){var len=Math.hypot(a[j].x-a[i].x,a[j].y-a[i].y);var label=fmt(len,2)+' m';out+='<rect x="'+(mx-27)+'" y="'+(my-11)+'" width="54" height="16" rx="5" fill="#ffffff" fill-opacity="0.88" stroke="#9aae99" stroke-width="0.7"/><text x="'+mx+'" y="'+(my+1)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" font-weight="700" fill="#36533a">'+label+'</text>';} }
 if(splits.length&&areaSplitMeta&&areaSplitMeta.cuts&&areaSplitMeta.cuts.length){
   function axisHits(axis,value){
     var hits=[];
     for(var k=0;k<a.length;k++){
       var u=a[k],v=a[(k+1)%a.length],du=v[axis]-u[axis];
       if(Math.abs(du)<1e-12)continue;
       var t=(value-u[axis])/du;
       if(t>=-1e-9&&t<=1+1e-9)hits.push({x:u.x+(v.x-u.x)*t,y:u.y+(v.y-u.y)*t});
     }
     return hits;
   }
   areaSplitMeta.cuts.forEach(function(cut){
     var hits=axisHits(areaSplitMeta.axis,cut);
     if(hits.length>=2){
       if(areaSplitMeta.axis==='x'){
         hits.sort(function(u,v){return u.y-v.y;});
       }else{
         hits.sort(function(u,v){return u.x-v.x;});
       }
       var u=hits[0],v=hits[hits.length-1];
       out+='<line x1="'+sx(u.x)+'" y1="'+sy(u.y)+'" x2="'+sx(v.x)+'" y2="'+sy(v.y)+'" stroke="#245d2c" stroke-width="3" stroke-dasharray="10 6"/>';
     }
   });
   function polyCentroid(q){
     var ss=0,cx=0,cy=0;
     for(var k=0;k<q.length;k++){var l=(k+1)%q.length,cr=q[k].x*q[l].y-q[l].x*q[k].y;ss+=cr;cx+=(q[k].x+q[l].x)*cr;cy+=(q[k].y+q[l].y)*cr;}
     if(Math.abs(ss)<1e-12)return {x:q[0].x,y:q[0].y};
     return {x:cx/(3*ss),y:cy/(3*ss)};
   }
   splits.forEach(function(q,i){
     if(!q||q.length<3)return;
     var c=polyCentroid(q),cx=sx(c.x),cy=sy(c.y),ar=areaPolyArea(q);
     var txt='قطعه '+(i+1)+'\n'+fmt(ar,2)+' m²';
     var parts=txt.split('\n'),bw=92,bh=34;
     out+='<rect x="'+(cx-bw/2)+'" y="'+(cy-bh/2)+'" width="'+bw+'" height="'+bh+'" rx="8" fill="#ffffff" fill-opacity="0.94" stroke="#5c805f" stroke-width="1.2"/>';
     out+='<text x="'+cx+'" y="'+(cy-4)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="900" fill="#245d2c">'+parts[0]+'</text>';
     out+='<text x="'+cx+'" y="'+(cy+11)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" font-weight="700" fill="#36533a">'+parts[1]+'</text>';
   });
 }
 if(off)out+='<text x="w" y="w"/>';
 out+='<text x="660" y="315" text-anchor="end" font-size="10" fill="#4f5e50">'+(off?'مرز Offset با خط‌چین نمایش داده می‌شود':(splits.length?'خط تقسیم و مساحت قطعات نمایش داده می‌شود':''))+'</text>';
 svg.innerHTML=out;
 if($("areaMapScale"))$("areaMapScale").textContent='مقیاس نمایشی: '+fmt(1/scale,2)+' m/px';
}
function areaClearOutputs(){areaLastPoints=null;areaLastStats=null;areaOffsetLast=null;areaSplitLast=null;areaSplitMeta=null;$("areaValidation").className='area-validation neutral';$("areaValidation").textContent='پس از محاسبه، اعتبار هندسی پلیگون بررسی می‌شود.';$("areaResult").innerHTML='<div class="area-empty-result">حداقل ۳ نقطه وارد کن و «محاسبه» را بزن.</div>';$("areaUnitSummary").innerHTML='';$("areaSides").innerHTML='<div class="area-empty-result">بعد از محاسبه، طول و آزیموت هر ضلع نمایش داده می‌شود.</div>';drawAreaSketch();}
function clearAreaPoints(){areaPointCount=3;renderAreaPoints();areaClearOutputs();return false;}
function areaFileName(ext){var n=(job&&job.name?job.name:'Area_Polygon').replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi,'_');return n+'_AREA.'+ext;}
function areaEnsurePoints(){if(!areaLastPoints){if(!calculateArea())return false;}return !!areaLastPoints;}
function areaExportCSV(){if(!areaEnsurePoints())return false;var t='Point,X,Y\n'+areaLastPoints.map(function(p){return 'P'+p.no+','+p.x+','+p.y}).join('\n');saveBlobFile(areaFileName('csv'),new Blob([t],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'}));return false;}
function areaExportTXT(){if(!areaEnsurePoints())return false;var s='MILADGEO — AREA REPORT\nJOB: '+((job&&job.name)||'—')+'\nAREA: '+fmt(areaLastStats.area,3)+' m2\nPERIMETER: '+fmt(areaLastStats.per,3)+' m\n\nPOINTS\n';areaLastPoints.forEach(function(p){s+='P'+p.no+' X='+p.x+' Y='+p.y+'\n'});s+='\nSIDES\n';areaLastStats.segments.forEach(function(q,i){s+=(i+1)+': P'+q.from+' -> P'+q.to+' | '+fmt(q.length,3)+' m | Az '+fmt(q.azimuth,4)+' deg\n'});var blob=new Blob([s],{type:'text/plain;charset=utf-8'});return saveBlobLikeMenu17(areaFileName('txt'),blob);}
function areaSvgForReport(){
 var svg=$("areaSketch");
 if(!svg)return '';
 try{return new XMLSerializer().serializeToString(svg);}catch(e){return ''}
}
function areaPrintReport(){
 if(!areaEnsurePoints())return false;
 try{
  var svg=areaSvgForReport();
  var html='<div dir="rtl" style="font-family:Arial,Tahoma,sans-serif;color:#172018;padding:8px">'+
    '<h1 style="font-size:22px;text-align:center;margin:0 0 12px">گزارش حرفه‌ای مساحت — MiladGeo</h1>'+
    '<div style="padding:12px;background:#f3f6f2;border:1px solid #ccd5ca;border-radius:8px;line-height:2">'+
    '<b>JOB:</b> '+escapeHTML((job&&job.name)||'—')+'<br>'+
    '<b>مساحت:</b> '+fmt(areaLastStats.area,3)+' m²<br>'+
    '<b>محیط:</b> '+fmt(areaLastStats.per,3)+' m<br>'+
    '<b>تعداد نقاط:</b> '+areaLastPoints.length+'<br>'+
    '<b>مرکز:</b> X='+fmt(areaLastStats.centroid.x,3)+' | Y='+fmt(areaLastStats.centroid.y,3)+
    '</div>'+ 
    '<div style="margin:16px 0;border:1px solid #aeb8ad;padding:8px;border-radius:8px;page-break-inside:avoid"><div style="font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:8px">شکل پلیگون</div>'+svg+'</div>'+ 
    '<h3>نقاط</h3><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse"><tr><th style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;background:#eef2ed">نقطه</th><th style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;background:#eef2ed">X</th><th style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;background:#eef2ed">Y</th></tr>'+areaLastPoints.map(function(p){return '<tr><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;text-align:center">P'+p.no+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;text-align:center">'+p.x+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;text-align:center">'+p.y+'</td></tr>';}).join('')+'</table>'+ 
    '<h3>اضلاع</h3><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse"><tr><th style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;background:#eef2ed">ضلع</th><th style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;background:#eef2ed">از</th><th style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;background:#eef2ed">به</th><th style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;background:#eef2ed">طول</th><th style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;background:#eef2ed">آزیموت</th></tr>'+areaLastStats.segments.map(function(q,i){return '<tr><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;text-align:center">'+(i+1)+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;text-align:center">P'+q.from+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;text-align:center">P'+q.to+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;text-align:center">'+fmt(q.length,3)+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;padding:6px;text-align:center">'+fmt(q.azimuth,4)+'</td></tr>';}).join('')+'</table></div>';
  if(typeof window.miladgeoM17PrintHtml==='function'){
   var printed=window.miladgeoM17PrintHtml(html,'MiladGeo — گزارش حرفه‌ای مساحت');
   if(printed!==false){showToast('✓ گزارش PDF مساحت آماده است');return false;}
  }
  showToast('موتور PDF در دسترس نیست');
 }catch(e){console.error('AREA_PDF',e);showToast('ساخت PDF مساحت ناموفق بود');}
 return false;
}
var areaOffsetLast=null,areaSplitLast=null,areaSplitMeta=null;
function areaLineIntersection(a,b,c,d){var x1=a.x,y1=a.y,x2=b.x,y2=b.y,x3=c.x,y3=c.y,x4=d.x,y4=d.y;var den=(x1-x2)*(y3-y4)-(y1-y2)*(x3-x4);if(Math.abs(den)<1e-12)return null;var px=((x1*y2-y1*x2)*(x3-x4)-(x1-x2)*(x3*y4-y3*x4))/den;var py=((x1*y2-y1*x2)*(y3-y4)-(y1-y2)*(x3*y4-y3*x4))/den;return {x:px,y:py};}
function areaOffsetPolygon(a,d){var n=a.length;if(n<3)return [];var sgn=0;for(var i=0;i<n;i++){var j=(i+1)%n;sgn+=a[i].x*a[j].y-a[j].x*a[i].y;}var ccw=sgn>=0,out=[];for(var i=0;i<n;i++){var ip=(i-1+n)%n,ic=i,inn=(i+1)%n;function shifted(u,v){var dx=v.x-u.x,dy=v.y-u.y,L=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(L<1e-12)return null;var nx=ccw?dy/L:-dy/L,ny=ccw?-dx/L:dx/L;return [{x:u.x+nx*d,y:u.y+ny*d},{x:v.x+nx*d,y:v.y+ny*d}];}var e1=shifted(a[ip],a[ic]),e2=shifted(a[ic],a[inn]);if(!e1||!e2)return null;var q=areaLineIntersection(e1[0],e1[1],e2[0],e2[1]);if(!q)return null;out.push(q);}return out;}
function areaRenderExtraPolys(){
 drawAreaSketch();
 var box=$("areaSplitResult");
 if(!box)return;
 if(!areaSplitLast||!areaSplitLast.length){box.style.display='none';box.innerHTML='';var out0=$('areaSplitOutput');if(out0)out0.style.display='none';return;}
 var total=areaPolyArea(areaLastPoints),meta=areaSplitMeta||{mode:'equal',axis:'x',targets:[]},h='<div style="font-weight:1000;text-align:center;margin-bottom:6px">نتیجه تقسیم زمین</div><div class="area-stat-grid">';
 areaSplitLast.forEach(function(q,i){var ar=areaPolyArea(q),pct=total?ar/total*100:0,target=meta.targets&&meta.targets[i]?meta.targets[i]:ar,err=ar-target;h+='<div class="area-stat"><small>قطعه '+(i+1)+'</small><b>'+fmt(ar,2)+' m²</b><small>'+fmt(pct,2)+'٪'+(meta.mode==='custom'?'<br>هدف: '+fmt(target,2)+' m²<br>اختلاف: '+fmt(err,2)+' m²':'')+'</small></div>';});
 var diff=0;if(meta.mode==='custom'){for(var i=0;i<areaSplitLast.length;i++){var ar=areaPolyArea(areaSplitLast[i]);diff=Math.max(diff,Math.abs(ar-(meta.targets[i]||ar)));}}
 h+='</div><div class="area-split-coords" style="margin-top:10px"><div style="font-weight:1000;text-align:center;margin-bottom:7px">مختصات قطعات تقسیم‌شده</div>';
 areaSplitLast.forEach(function(q,pi){h+='<div style="border:1px solid #ccd7c9;border-radius:10px;padding:7px;margin:6px 0;background:#fbfdfb"><div style="font-weight:900;margin-bottom:5px">قطعه '+(pi+1)+' — '+fmt(areaPolyArea(q),2)+' m²</div><div style="overflow:auto"><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px"><tr><th style="padding:4px;border-bottom:1px solid #d7ded5">نقطه</th><th style="padding:4px;border-bottom:1px solid #d7ded5">X / Easting</th><th style="padding:4px;border-bottom:1px solid #d7ded5">Y / Northing</th></tr>';q.forEach(function(pt,vi){h+='<tr><td style="padding:4px;text-align:center">P'+(vi+1)+'</td><td style="padding:4px;text-align:center">'+Number(pt.x).toFixed(2)+'</td><td style="padding:4px;text-align:center">'+Number(pt.y).toFixed(2)+'</td></tr>';});h+='</table></div></div>';});
 h+='</div><div class="area-pro-help" style="text-align:center;margin-top:7px">مساحت کل: '+fmt(total,2)+' m² &nbsp;|&nbsp; تعداد قطعات: '+areaSplitLast.length+' &nbsp;|&nbsp; جهت: '+String(meta.axis).toUpperCase()+(meta.mode==='custom'?' &nbsp;|&nbsp; بیشترین اختلاف با هدف: '+fmt(diff,2)+' m²':'')+'</div>';
 box.innerHTML=h;box.style.display='block';
 var out=$('areaSplitOutput');if(out)out.style.display='block';
}
function areaSplitEnsure(){if(!areaSplitLast||!areaSplitLast.length){showToast('ابتدا یک تقسیم زمین انجام بده');return false;}return true;}
function areaSplitRows(){var rows=[],meta=areaSplitMeta||{axis:'x',targets:[]};for(var i=0;i<areaSplitLast.length;i++){var q=areaSplitLast[i],ar=areaPolyArea(q),t=meta.targets&&meta.targets[i]?meta.targets[i]:ar;rows.push({part:i+1,area:ar,pct:areaLastStats&&areaLastStats.area?ar/areaLastStats.area*100:0,target:t,diff:ar-t,points:q});}return rows;}
function areaSplitSaveFile(name,blob,label){
 try{
  if(window.AndroidBridge&&typeof window.miladgeoDirectSaveBlob==='function'){
   var ok=window.miladgeoDirectSaveBlob(name,blob);if(ok!==false)return true;
  }
 }catch(e){console.error('AREA_SPLIT_NATIVE',e)}
 try{
  if(typeof window.m275SaveFile==='function')return window.m275SaveFile(name,blob,label||'خروجی');
 }catch(e){console.error('AREA_SPLIT_M275',e)}
 try{
  if(navigator.share&&typeof File!=='undefined'){
   var f=new File([blob],name,{type:blob.type||'application/octet-stream'});
   if(!navigator.canShare||navigator.canShare({files:[f]})){
    navigator.share({files:[f],title:'MiladGeo — '+(label||'خروجی تقسیم')}).then(function(){showToast('✓ فایل آماده شد')}).catch(function(){try{saveBlobFile(name,blob)}catch(_){}});
    return true;
   }
  }
 }catch(e){console.error('AREA_SPLIT_SHARE',e)}
 try{return saveBlobFile(name,blob)}catch(e){showToast('خروجی '+(label||'فایل')+' ناموفق بود');return false;}
}

function areaSplitExportDXF(){if(!areaSplitEnsure())return false;var out=['0','SECTION','2','ENTITIES'];areaSplitLast.forEach(function(q,pi){for(var i=0;i<q.length;i++){var j=(i+1)%q.length;out.push('0','LINE','8','SPLIT_'+(pi+1),'10',String(q[i].x),'20',String(q[i].y),'30','0','11',String(q[j].x),'21',String(q[j].y),'31','0');}});out.push('0','ENDSEC','0','EOF');return areaSplitSaveFile(areaFileName('split.dxf'),new Blob([out.join('\r\n')+'\r\n'],{type:'application/dxf'}),'DXF');}
function areaSplitExportKML(){if(!areaSplitEnsure())return false;var mode=$('areaKmlMode')?$('areaKmlMode').value:'utm',zone=$('areaKmlZone')?parseInt($('areaKmlZone').value,10):0,hem=$('areaKmlHem')?$('areaKmlHem').value:'N';if(mode==='utm'&&(!zone||zone<1||zone>60)){showToast('Zone معتبر KML را وارد کن');return false;}var plac='';areaSplitLast.forEach(function(q,pi){var coords=[];q.forEach(function(p){var ll=mode==='utm'&&typeof utmToLatLon==='function'?utmToLatLon(p.x,p.y,zone,hem==='N'):{lat:p.y,lng:p.x};coords.push(Number((ll.lon!=null?ll.lon:ll.lng)).toFixed(8)+','+Number(ll.lat).toFixed(8)+',0');});if(q.length)coords.push(coords[0]);plac+='<Placemark><name>قطعه '+(pi+1)+'</name><description>مساحت: '+areaPolyArea(q).toFixed(2)+' m2</description><Polygon><outerBoundaryIs><LinearRing><coordinates>'+coords.join(' ')+'</coordinates></LinearRing></outerBoundaryIs></Polygon></Placemark>';});var k='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><kml xmlns="http://www.opengis.net/kml/2.2"><Document><name>MiladGeo Split</name>'+plac+'</Document></kml>';return areaSplitSaveFile(areaFileName('split.kml'),new Blob([k],{type:'application/vnd.google-earth.kml+xml'}),'KML');}
function areaSplitMakePDFBlob(){return new Promise(function(resolve,reject){try{var rows=areaSplitRows(),lines=['MILADGEO - LAND SPLIT REPORT','Total Area: '+areaPolyArea(areaLastPoints).toFixed(2)+' m2','Parts: '+rows.length,'Direction: '+String((areaSplitMeta||{}).axis||'x').toUpperCase(),'','Part | Area m2 | Percent | Target m2 | Difference m2'];rows.forEach(function(r){lines.push(String(r.part)+' | '+r.area.toFixed(2)+' | '+r.pct.toFixed(2)+'% | '+r.target.toFixed(2)+' | '+r.diff.toFixed(2));});rows.forEach(function(r){lines.push('','PART '+r.part);r.points.forEach(function(p,i){lines.push('P'+(i+1)+'  X='+Number(p.x).toFixed(2)+'  Y='+Number(p.y).toFixed(2));});});var W=1240,H=1754,margin=70,lineH=36,font='28px Arial, Tahoma, sans-serif',canvas=document.createElement('canvas'),ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');canvas.width=W;canvas.height=H;ctx.font=font;ctx.textBaseline='top';var expanded=[];lines.forEach(function(line){var cur='',parts=String(line).split(/\s+/),max=W-margin*2;parts.forEach(function(word){var test=cur?cur+' '+word:word;if(ctx.measureText(test).width<=max)cur=test;else{if(cur)expanded.push(cur);cur=word;}});expanded.push(cur);});var per=Math.max(1,Math.floor((H-margin*2)/lineH)),count=Math.ceil(expanded.length/per),jpegs=[];function page(i){ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);ctx.fillStyle='#111';ctx.font=font;ctx.direction='ltr';ctx.textAlign='left';var start=i*per,end=Math.min(expanded.length,start+per);for(var j=start;j<end;j++)ctx.fillText(expanded[j],margin,margin+(j-start)*lineH);canvas.toBlob(function(blob){if(!blob){reject(Error('PDF_IMAGE'));return;}var fr=new FileReader();fr.onload=function(){jpegs.push(new Uint8Array(fr.result));if(jpegs.length===count){try{resolve(new Blob([m16BuildPdfFromJpegs(jpegs,W,H)],{type:'application/pdf'}));}catch(e){reject(e)}}else page(i+1)};fr.readAsArrayBuffer(blob)},'image/jpeg',0.9)}page(0)}catch(e){reject(e)}})}
function areaSplitPrintReport(){
 if(!areaSplitEnsure())return false;
 try{
  var rows=areaSplitRows(),meta=areaSplitMeta||{},jobName=(typeof job!=='undefined'&&job&&job.name)?String(job.name):'—';
  var esc=function(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;');};
  var h='<div dir="rtl" style="font-family:Arial,Tahoma,sans-serif;color:#111">'+
    '<h1 style="text-align:center;margin:0 0 12px">گزارش حرفه‌ای تقسیم زمین — MiladGeo</h1>'+
    '<div style="border:1px solid #777;padding:10px;margin-bottom:12px">'+
    '<b>JOB:</b> '+esc(jobName)+' &nbsp; | &nbsp; <b>مساحت کل:</b> '+areaPolyArea(areaLastPoints).toFixed(2)+' m² &nbsp; | &nbsp; <b>تعداد قطعات:</b> '+rows.length+' &nbsp; | &nbsp; <b>جهت:</b> '+esc(String(meta.axis||'x').toUpperCase())+''+
    '</div><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px"><thead><tr>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">قطعه</th><th style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">مساحت (m²)</th><th style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">درصد</th><th style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">مساحت هدف</th><th style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">اختلاف</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  rows.forEach(function(r){h+='<tr><td style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px;text-align:center">'+r.part+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">'+r.area.toFixed(2)+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">'+r.pct.toFixed(2)+'%</td><td style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">'+r.target.toFixed(2)+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #777;padding:6px">'+r.diff.toFixed(2)+'</td></tr>';});
  h+='</tbody></table>';
  var splitSvg='';
  try{
   var splitSvgEl=document.getElementById('areaSketch');
   if(splitSvgEl&&splitSvgEl.outerHTML){
    splitSvg='<div style="margin:16px 0 18px;border:1px solid #9aae99;border-radius:12px;padding:10px;background:#f7faf6;page-break-inside:avoid">'+
      '<h2 style="text-align:center;margin:0 0 8px;font-size:18px">نقشه شماتیک تقسیم زمین</h2>'+
      '<div style="text-align:center">'+splitSvgEl.outerHTML.replace('<svg ','<svg style=\"width:100%;height:auto;max-height:420px;\" ')+ '</div>'+
      '<div style="text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:#566356;margin-top:6px">خط‌های تقسیم و مساحت قطعات روی نقشه نمایش داده شده‌اند.</div>'+
      '</div>';
   }
  }catch(e){console.warn('AREA_SPLIT_PDF_SKETCH',e)}
  h+=splitSvg;
  rows.forEach(function(r){h+='<h3 style="margin:16px 0 6px">مختصات قطعه '+r.part+' — '+r.area.toFixed(2)+' m²</h3><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11px"><thead><tr><th style="border:1px solid #777;padding:5px">نقطه</th><th style="border:1px solid #777;padding:5px">X</th><th style="border:1px solid #777;padding:5px">Y</th></tr></thead><tbody>';r.points.forEach(function(pt,i){h+='<tr><td style="border:1px solid #777;padding:5px">P'+(i+1)+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #777;padding:5px">'+Number(pt.x).toFixed(2)+'</td><td style="border:1px solid #777;padding:5px">'+Number(pt.y).toFixed(2)+'</td></tr>';});h+='</tbody></table>';});
  h+='</div>';
  if(typeof window.miladgeoM17PrintHtml==='function'){
   window.miladgeoM17PrintHtml(h,'MiladGeo — گزارش تقسیم زمین');
   showToast('✓ گزارش PDF تقسیم آماده است');
   return false;
  }
  showToast('موتور PDF در دسترس نیست');
 }catch(e){console.error('AREA_SPLIT_PDF',e);showToast('ساخت PDF ناموفق بود');}
 return false;
}
function areaOffset(){if(!areaEnsurePoints())return false;var d=parseFloat(window.prompt('فاصله Offset / Buffer را بر حسب متر وارد کنید:','5'));if(!isFinite(d)||d<=0){showToast('فاصله معتبر وارد کن');return false;}var off=areaOffsetPolygon(areaLastPoints,d);if(!off||off.length!==areaLastPoints.length){showToast('ساخت Offset دقیق برای این هندسه ممکن نشد');return false;}areaOffsetLast=off;areaSplitLast=null;areaRenderExtraPolys();showToast('Offset دقیق با فاصله '+fmt(d,2)+' متر ساخته شد ✓');return false;}
function areaClipAxis(poly,axis,value,keepLow){var out=[];if(!poly.length)return out;function inside(p){return keepLow?p[axis]<=value+1e-9:p[axis]>=value-1e-9;}function intersect(a,b){var da=b[axis]-a[axis];if(Math.abs(da)<1e-12)return {x:a.x,y:a.y};var t=(value-a[axis])/da;return {x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t};}for(var i=0;i<poly.length;i++){var a=poly[i],b=poly[(i+1)%poly.length],ia=inside(a),ib=inside(b);if(ia&&ib)out.push({x:b.x,y:b.y});else if(ia&&!ib)out.push(intersect(a,b));else if(!ia&&ib){out.push(intersect(a,b));out.push({x:b.x,y:b.y});}}return out;}
function areaPolyArea(p){if(p.length<3)return 0;var s=0;for(var i=0;i<p.length;i++){var j=(i+1)%p.length;s+=p[i].x*p[j].y-p[j].x*p[i].y;}return Math.abs(s)/2;}
function areaSplit(){if(!areaEnsurePoints())return false;var n=parseInt(window.prompt('زمین به چند قسمت مساوی تقسیم شود؟ (۲ تا ۱۰)','2'),10);if(!isFinite(n)||n<2||n>10){showToast('تعداد قسمت باید بین ۲ تا ۱۰ باشد');return false;}var axis=(window.prompt('جهت تقسیم را وارد کن: X یا Y','X')||'X').toUpperCase()==='Y'?'y':'x';var a=areaLastPoints,total=areaPolyArea(a),vals=a.map(function(p){return p[axis]}),lo=Math.min.apply(null,vals),hi=Math.max.apply(null,vals),parts=[],cuts=[];for(var k=1;k<n;k++){var target=total*k/n,left=lo,right=hi,c=lo;for(var it=0;it<55;it++){c=(left+right)/2;var low=areaClipAxis(a,axis,c,true),aa=areaPolyArea(low);if(aa<target)left=c;else right=c;}cuts.push(c);}var bounds=[lo].concat(cuts).concat([hi]);for(var z=0;z<n;z++){var low=bounds[z],high=bounds[z+1],p1=areaClipAxis(a,axis,high,true),p2=areaClipAxis(p1,axis,low,false);if(p2.length>=3&&areaPolyArea(p2)>1e-9)parts.push(p2);}if(parts.length!==n){showToast('برای این شکل، تقسیم مساوی در این جهت ممکن نشد؛ جهت دیگر را امتحان کن');return false;}areaSplitLast=parts;areaSplitMeta={mode:'equal',axis:axis,targets:parts.map(function(q){return total/n}),cuts:cuts};areaOffsetLast=null;areaRenderExtraPolys();showToast('زمین به '+n+' قسمت تقریباً مساوی تقسیم شد ✓');return false;}
function areaSplitByArea(){
 if(!areaEnsurePoints())return false;
 var a=areaLastPoints,total=areaPolyArea(a);
 if(!isFinite(total)||total<=0){showToast('مساحت پلیگون معتبر نیست');return false;}
 var n=parseInt(window.prompt('تعداد قطعات را وارد کنید (۲ تا ۱۰):','2'),10);
 if(!isFinite(n)||n<2||n>10){showToast('تعداد قسمت باید بین ۲ تا ۱۰ باشد');return false;}
 var raw=window.prompt('مساحت قطعات را با ویرگول یا فاصله وارد کنید. برای ۲ قطعه فقط مساحت قطعه اول را بنویس؛ قطعه دوم از باقیمانده محاسبه می‌شود. برای چند قطعه می‌توانی n−1 مقدار بدهی و قطعه آخر خودکار محاسبه شود. مثال: 130, 270','');
 if(raw===null)return false;
 function normNum(v){
   var t=String(v==null?'':v).trim()
     .replace(/[۰-۹]/g,function(ch){return String('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'.indexOf(ch))})
     .replace(/[٠-٩]/g,function(ch){return String('٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(ch))})
     .replace(/[٬،]/g,',').replace(/\s/g,'');
   if(t.indexOf(',')>=0&&t.indexOf('.')<0)t=t.replace(',', '.');
   else t=t.replace(/,/g,'');
   return parseFloat(t);
 }
 var tokens=String(raw).trim().split(/[;,|\n\s]+/).filter(function(v){return String(v).trim()!=='';}),vals=[];
 for(var vi=0;vi<tokens.length;vi++){var nv=normNum(tokens[vi]);if(isFinite(nv)&&nv>0)vals.push(nv);}
 if(vals.length!==n-1&&vals.length!==n){showToast('برای '+n+' قطعه، '+(n-1)+' مساحت اول یا '+n+' مساحت کامل را وارد کن');return false;}
 if(vals.some(function(v){return !isFinite(v)||v<=0;})){showToast('همه مساحت‌ها باید عدد مثبت باشند');return false;}
 var sum=vals.reduce(function(x,v){return x+v},0),tol=Math.max(0.01,total*1e-6);
 if(vals.length===n){if(Math.abs(sum-total)>tol){showToast('مجموع مساحت‌ها باید با مساحت کل برابر باشد؛ اختلاف: '+fmt(Math.abs(sum-total),2)+' m²');return false;}}
 else {var last=total-sum;if(last<=tol){showToast('مجموع مساحت‌های واردشده باید کمتر از مساحت کل باشد');return false;}vals.push(last);}
 var axis=((window.prompt('جهت تقسیم را وارد کن: X یا Y','X')||'X').trim().toUpperCase()==='Y')?'y':'x';
 var coords=a.map(function(p){return Number(p[axis]);}),lo=Math.min.apply(null,coords),hi=Math.max.apply(null,coords);
 var remaining=a.map(function(p){return {x:p.x,y:p.y}}),parts=[],targets=[],cuts=[],cursor=lo;
 for(var k=0;k<n-1;k++){
   var target=vals[k],remainingArea=areaPolyArea(remaining);
   if(target<=tol||target>=remainingArea-tol){showToast('مساحت قطعه '+(k+1)+' قابل ساخت نیست؛ مقدار دیگری وارد کن');return false;}
   var rlo=cursor,rhi=hi,c=rlo;
   for(var it=0;it<70;it++){
     c=(rlo+rhi)/2;
     var low=areaClipAxis(remaining,axis,c,true),aa=areaPolyArea(low);
     if(aa<target)rlo=c;else rhi=c;
   }
   c=(rlo+rhi)/2;
   var piece=areaClipAxis(remaining,axis,c,true),next=areaClipAxis(remaining,axis,c,false);
   if(piece.length<3||next.length<3){showToast('برای این شکل و جهت انتخاب‌شده، این مساحت قابل ساخت نیست؛ جهت دیگر را امتحان کن');return false;}
   var actual=areaPolyArea(piece);
   if(Math.abs(actual-target)>Math.max(0.01,total*1e-7)){showToast('دقت تقسیم کافی نیست؛ جهت دیگر را امتحان کن');return false;}
   parts.push(piece);targets.push(target);cuts.push(c);remaining=next;cursor=c;
 }
 parts.push(remaining);targets.push(areaPolyArea(remaining));
 if(parts.length!==n){showToast('تقسیم با مساحت دلخواه انجام نشد');return false;}
 areaSplitLast=parts;
 areaSplitMeta={mode:'custom',axis:axis,targets:targets,cuts:cuts};
 areaOffsetLast=null;areaRenderExtraPolys();
 showToast('تقسیم زمین بر اساس مساحت دلخواه انجام شد ✓');return false;
}
function isTelegramMiniApp(){return !!(window.Telegram&&window.Telegram.WebApp&&window.Telegram.WebApp.initData);}
function telegramWebApp(){return isTelegramMiniApp()?window.Telegram.WebApp:null;}
function saveBlobFile(name,blob){
 /* V56: replace tmpfiles.org page-link handling with TempFile.org direct-download API.
    Telegram needs a real HTTPS file URL; the old gateway could return an HTML page. */
 name=String(name||'MILADGEO_EXPORT.bin').replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]+/g,'_');
 /* Native Android WebView: route every generated file (TXT/CSV/XLS/XLSX/PDF/DXF/KML/KMZ/JSON) through Android's Save As dialog. */
 try{
  if(window.AndroidBridge&&typeof window.AndroidBridge.saveFile==='function'&&typeof directSaveBlob==='function'&&blob){
   return directSaveBlob(name,blob);
  }
 }catch(nativeSaveError){console.error('Native file save bridge unavailable',nativeSaveError);}
 var type=blob&&blob.type?blob.type:'application/octet-stream';
 var tg=telegramWebApp();
 var file;
 try{file=new File([blob],name,{type:type});}catch(e){file=null;}

 if(tg&&typeof tg.downloadFile==='function'&&file){
  showToast('⏳ آماده‌سازی فایل...');
  try{
   var fd=new FormData();
   fd.append('files',file,name);
   fd.append('expiryHours','24');
   fetch('https://tempfile.org/api/upload/local',{method:'POST',body:fd,mode:'cors'})
    .then(function(r){
      if(!r.ok)throw new Error('UPLOAD_HTTP_'+r.status);
      return r.json();
    })
    .then(function(data){
      if(!data||!data.success||!data.files||!data.files.length)throw new Error((data&&data.error)||'UPLOAD_NO_FILE');
      var f=data.files[0]||{};
      var id=String(f.id||'');
      if(!id)throw new Error('UPLOAD_NO_ID');
      var url='https://tempfile.org/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/download';
      try{
       if(tg.onEvent)tg.onEvent('fileDownloadRequested',function(ev){
        if(ev&&ev.status==='downloading')showToast('✓ دانلود شروع شد');
        else if(ev&&ev.status==='cancelled')showToast('دانلود لغو شد');
       });
      }catch(e){}
      tg.downloadFile({url:url,file_name:name},function(ok){
       if(ok)showToast('✓ دانلود شروع شد');
       else{
        showToast('⚠️ دانلود Telegram ناموفق؛ باز کردن فایل...');
        try{if(tg.openLink)tg.openLink(url);else window.open(url,'_blank');}catch(e){try{window.open(url,'_blank');}catch(x){}}
       }
      });
    })
    .catch(function(err){
      console.error('Telegram export upload/download failed',err);
      /* Share the ORIGINAL blob, never the upload-service HTML page. */
      if(file&&navigator.share){
       try{
        var can=!navigator.canShare||navigator.canShare({files:[file]});
        if(can){navigator.share({files:[file],title:name,text:'MiladGeo export'}).then(function(){showToast('✓ فایل آماده اشتراک‌گذاری است');}).catch(function(){browserSaveBlob(name,blob,tg);});return;}
       }catch(e){}
      }
      browserSaveBlob(name,blob,tg);
    });
   return true;
  }catch(e){console.error(e);}
 }

 /* Browser / non-Telegram: save the actual Blob directly. */
 if(!tg&&window.showSaveFilePicker&&file){
  try{
   window.showSaveFilePicker({suggestedName:name,types:[{description:'MiladGeo file',accept:{[type]:[getFileExtension(name)]}}]}).then(function(handle){
    return handle.createWritable().then(function(w){return w.write(blob).then(function(){return w.close();});});
   }).then(function(){showToast('✓ File saved');}).catch(function(err){if(!(err&&err.name==='AbortError'))browserSaveBlob(name,blob,false);});
   return true;
  }catch(e){}
 }
 return browserSaveBlob(name,blob,tg);
}
function getFileExtension(name){var m=String(name).match(/\.[A-Za-z0-9]+$/);return m?m[0]:'.bin';}
function browserSaveBlob(name,blob,tg){
 try{
  var url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;a.download=name;a.rel='noopener';a.style.display='none';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();
  setTimeout(function(){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(url);}catch(e){}try{a.remove();}catch(e){}},10000);
  showToast(tg?'⚠️ Download fallback':'✓ Download started');
  return true;
 }catch(e){
  showToast('Export failed — use Share / Save');
  return false;
 }
}
function downloadTextFile(name,text,mime){return saveBlobFile(name,new Blob([text],{type:mime||'text/plain;charset=utf-8'}));}
function exportAreaDXF(){var a=getAreaPoints();if(!a)return false;var out=['0','SECTION','2','HEADER','0','ENDSEC','0','SECTION','2','ENTITIES'];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var j=(i+1)%a.length;out.push('0','LINE','8','AREA','10',String(a[i].x),'20',String(a[i].y),'30','0','11',String(a[j].x),'21',String(a[j].y),'31','0');out.push('0','POINT','8','POINTS','10',String(a[i].x),'20',String(a[i].y),'30','0');}out.push('0','ENDSEC','0','EOF');saveBlobLikeMenu17(areaFileName('dxf'),new Blob([out.join('\r\n')+'\r\n'],{type:'application/dxf'}));return false;}
function exportAreaExcel(){var a=getAreaPoints();if(!a)return false;var sum=0,per=0,rows='';for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var j=(i+1)%a.length,dx=a[j].x-a[i].x,dy=a[j].y-a[i].y,len=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy),az=Math.atan2(dx,dy)*180/Math.PI;if(az<0)az+=360;sum+=a[i].x*a[j].y-a[j].x*a[i].y;per+=len;rows+='<tr><td>P'+a[i].no+'</td><td>'+a[i].x+'</td><td>'+a[i].y+'</td><td>'+len.toFixed(3)+'</td><td>'+az.toFixed(3)+'</td></tr>';}var area=Math.abs(sum)/2;var html='<?xml version="1.0"?><Workbook xmlns="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet" xmlns:ss="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet"><Worksheet ss:Name="Area"><Table><Row><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">Point</Data></Cell><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">X</Data></Cell><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">Y</Data></Cell><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">Side Length</Data></Cell><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">Azimuth</Data></Cell></Row>'+rows+'<Row><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">AREA</Data></Cell><Cell><Data ss:Type="Number">'+area+'</Data></Cell><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">m2</Data></Cell></Row><Row><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">PERIMETER</Data></Cell><Cell><Data ss:Type="Number">'+per+'</Data></Cell><Cell><Data ss:Type="String">m</Data></Cell></Row></Table></Worksheet></Workbook>';saveBlobLikeMenu17(areaFileName('xls'),new Blob([html],{type:'application/vnd.ms-excel'}));return false;}
function utmToLatLon(easting,northing,zone,northern){
 var a=6378137.0, eccSquared=0.00669438, k0=0.9996;
 var eccPrimeSquared=eccSquared/(1-eccSquared);
 var e1=(1-Math.sqrt(1-eccSquared))/(1+Math.sqrt(1-eccSquared));
 var x=easting-500000.0, y=northing;
 if(!northern)y-=10000000.0;
 var longOrigin=(zone-1)*6-180+3;
 var M=y/k0;
 var mu=M/(a*(1-eccSquared/4-3*eccSquared*eccSquared/64-5*Math.pow(eccSquared,3)/256));
 var phi1Rad=mu+(3*e1/2-27*Math.pow(e1,3)/32)*Math.sin(2*mu)+(21*e1*e1/16-55*Math.pow(e1,4)/32)*Math.sin(4*mu)+(151*Math.pow(e1,3)/96)*Math.sin(6*mu)+(1097*Math.pow(e1,4)/512)*Math.sin(8*mu);
 var N1=a/Math.sqrt(1-eccSquared*Math.sin(phi1Rad)*Math.sin(phi1Rad));
 var T1=Math.tan(phi1Rad)*Math.tan(phi1Rad);
 var C1=eccPrimeSquared*Math.cos(phi1Rad)*Math.cos(phi1Rad);
 var R1=a*(1-eccSquared)/Math.pow(1-eccSquared*Math.sin(phi1Rad)*Math.sin(phi1Rad),1.5);
 var D=x/(N1*k0);
 var lat=phi1Rad-(N1*Math.tan(phi1Rad)/R1)*(D*D/2-(5+3*T1+10*C1-4*C1*C1-9*eccPrimeSquared)*Math.pow(D,4)/24+(61+90*T1+298*C1+45*T1*T1-252*eccPrimeSquared-3*C1*C1)*Math.pow(D,6)/720);
 var lon=(D-(1+2*T1+C1)*Math.pow(D,3)/6+(5-2*C1+28*T1-3*C1*C1+8*eccPrimeSquared+24*T1*T1)*Math.pow(D,5)/120)/Math.cos(phi1Rad);
 lat=lat*180/Math.PI; lon=longOrigin+lon*180/Math.PI;
 return {lat:lat,lon:lon};
}
function exportAreaKML(){
 var a=getAreaPoints();if(!a)return false;
 var mode=$("areaKmlMode")?$("areaKmlMode").value:'utm',zone=parseInt($("areaKmlZone")?$("areaKmlZone").value:'',10),northern=$("areaKmlHem")?$("areaKmlHem").value==='N':true,i,geoPts=[];
 if(mode==='geo'){
   for(i=0;i<a.length;i++){if(a[i].x<-180||a[i].x>180||a[i].y<-90||a[i].y>90){showToast('طول/عرض جغرافیایی خارج از محدوده است');return false;}geoPts.push({x:a[i].x,y:a[i].y});}
 }else{
   if(!isFinite(zone)||zone<1||zone>60){showToast('شماره Zone را وارد کن');return false;}
   for(i=0;i<a.length;i++){
     if(a[i].x<100000||a[i].x>900000||a[i].y<0||a[i].y>10000000){showToast('محدوده UTM نامعتبر است؛ X=Easting و Y=Northing');return false;}
     var ll=utmToLatLon(a[i].x,a[i].y,zone,northern);geoPts.push({x:ll.lon,y:ll.lat});
   }
 }
 var coords='';for(i=0;i<geoPts.length;i++)coords+=geoPts[i].x.toFixed(8)+','+geoPts[i].y.toFixed(8)+',0 ';coords+=geoPts[0].x.toFixed(8)+','+geoPts[0].y.toFixed(8)+',0';
 var name=(job&&job.name?job.name:'MILADGEO_AREA');var kml='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<kml xmlns="http://www.opengis.net/kml/2.2"><Document><name>'+escapeHTML(name)+' AREA</name><Placemark><name>AREA POLYGON</name><Style><LineStyle><width>3</width></LineStyle><PolyStyle><fill>0.35</fill><outline>1</outline></PolyStyle></Style><Polygon><outerBoundaryIs><LinearRing><coordinates>'+coords+'</coordinates></LinearRing></outerBoundaryIs></Polygon></Placemark></Document></kml>';
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(areaFileName('kml'),new Blob([kml],{type:'application/vnd.google-earth.kml+xml;charset=utf-8'}));showToast('KML آماده شد ✓');return false;
}

function clearAreaPoints(){areaPointCount=3;renderAreaPoints();var r=$("areaResult");if(r)r.innerHTML='';showToast('Area points cleared');return false;}
function getNextPointNumber(){var max=0,i,m,n;for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){m=String(points[i].no||'').match(/\d+$/);if(m){n=parseInt(m[0],10);if(n>max)max=n;}}return max+1;}
function prepareNewPoint(){$("pointNo").value='P'+('000'+getNextPointNumber()).slice(-3);$("pointDP").value='';$("distance").value='';$("pointAz").value='';$("dz").value='';$("hi").value=finite(Number(setup.stationHI))?setup.stationHI:'';$("pointResult").innerHTML='';}
function refreshLeicaLineSelects(){var a=$("linePointA"),b=$("linePointB");if(!a||!b)return;var oa=a.value,ob=b.value,opts='<option value="">— انتخاب نقطه —</option>';(Array.isArray(points)?points:[]).forEach(function(p){if(!p||!finite(Number(p.x))||!finite(Number(p.y)))return;opts+='<option value="'+escapeHTML(String(p.id))+'">'+escapeHTML(menu1PointLabel(p))+' — X '+fmt(p.x,2)+' | Y '+fmt(p.y,2)+'</option>';});a.innerHTML=opts;b.innerHTML=opts;if(setup.referenceLineSet){if(a.querySelector('option[value="'+CSS.escape(String(setup.referenceAId||''))+'"]'))a.value=String(setup.referenceAId);if(b.querySelector('option[value="'+CSS.escape(String(setup.referenceBId||''))+'"]'))b.value=String(setup.referenceBId);}else{if(oa&&a.querySelector('option[value="'+CSS.escape(oa)+'"]'))a.value=oa;if(ob&&b.querySelector('option[value="'+CSS.escape(ob)+'"]'))b.value=ob;}}
function renderLeicaLineUI(){refreshLeicaLineSelects();var r=$("leicaLineResult"),a=$("leicaLineActive");if(!r||!a)return;if(setup.referenceLineSet){r.innerHTML='<div class="leica-line-result"><div><small>ΔE</small><b>'+fmt(setup.referenceDE,3)+' m</b></div><div><small>ΔN</small><b>'+fmt(setup.referenceDN,3)+' m</b></div><div><small>LENGTH</small><b>'+fmt(setup.referenceLength,3)+' m</b></div><div><small>AZIMUTH</small><b>'+fmt(setup.referenceAzimuth,4)+'°</b></div></div>';a.innerHTML='<div class="menu1-ref-active">✓ '+escapeHTML(setup.referenceAName||'A')+' → '+escapeHTML(setup.referenceBName||'B')+' فعال است</div>';}else{r.innerHTML='';a.innerHTML='';}}
function setLeicaLineFromJob(){var aId=$("linePointA")?$("linePointA").value:'',bId=$("linePointB")?$("linePointB").value:'';if(!aId||!bId){showToast('نقطه A و B را انتخاب کن');return false;}if(String(aId)===String(bId)){showToast('A و B نباید یکسان باشند');return false;}var a=menu1FindPointById(aId),b=menu1FindPointById(bId);if(!a||!b){showToast('نقاط خط پیدا نشدند');return false;}var de=Number(b.x)-Number(a.x),dn=Number(b.y)-Number(a.y),len=Math.hypot(de,dn);if(!finite(len)||len<=0){showToast('طول خط معتبر نیست');return false;}setup.referenceLineSet=true;setup.referenceAId=String(a.id);setup.referenceBId=String(b.id);setup.referenceAName=menu1PointLabel(a);setup.referenceBName=menu1PointLabel(b);setup.referenceAX=Number(a.x);setup.referenceAY=Number(a.y);setup.referenceBX=Number(b.x);setup.referenceBY=Number(b.y);setup.referenceDE=de;setup.referenceDN=dn;setup.referenceLength=len;setup.referenceAzimuth=menu1GridAzimuth(de,dn);setup.referenceSource='job';saveJSON(SETUP_KEY,setup);renderReferenceLineUI();renderLeicaLineUI();if(typeof renderSurveyMap==='function')renderSurveyMap();showToast('REFERENCE LINE فعال شد ✓');return false;}
function setLeicaLineManual(){var ax=num($("lineAX").value),ay=num($("lineAY").value),bx=num($("lineBX").value),by=num($("lineBY").value);if(!finite(ax)||!finite(ay)||!finite(bx)||!finite(by)){showToast('مختصات A و B را کامل وارد کن');return false;}var de=bx-ax,dn=by-ay,len=Math.hypot(de,dn);if(!finite(len)||len<=0){showToast('طول خط معتبر نیست');return false;}setup.referenceLineSet=true;setup.referenceAId='';setup.referenceBId='';setup.referenceAName='A';setup.referenceBName='B';setup.referenceAX=ax;setup.referenceAY=ay;setup.referenceBX=bx;setup.referenceBY=by;setup.referenceDE=de;setup.referenceDN=dn;setup.referenceLength=len;setup.referenceAzimuth=menu1GridAzimuth(de,dn);setup.referenceSource='manual';saveJSON(SETUP_KEY,setup);renderReferenceLineUI();renderLeicaLineUI();if(typeof renderSurveyMap==='function')renderSurveyMap();showToast('خط دستی فعال شد ✓');return false;}
function calculatePoint(){if(!setupComplete()){showToast('Setup is not complete');return false;}var no=$("pointNo").value.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,''),dp=$("pointDP").value.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,''),d=num($("distance").value),hv=num($("pointAz").value),mode=$("measureAngleMode")?$("measureAngleMode").value:'hz',dh=num($("dz").value)||0,h=num($("hi").value);if(!finite(d)){showToast('Enter Distance');return false;}if(!finite(h))h=Number(setup.stationHI)||0;if(!finite(hv)){showToast('Enter Hz / Azimuth');return false;}var a=mode==='hz'?normalizeAzimuth(Number(setup.azimuth)+hv):normalizeAzimuth(hv),r=a*Math.PI/180,x=Number(setup.sx)+d*Math.sin(r),y=Number(setup.sy)+d*Math.cos(r),z=Number(setup.sz)+dh+h;lastCalculatedPoint={id:new Date().getTime(),no:no,dp:dp,x:x,y:y,z:z,distance:d,azimuth:a,rawHz:hv,angleMode:mode};$("pointResult").innerHTML='<div class="result"><div class="result-line"><span>Point</span><b>'+escapeHTML(no)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>X</span><b>'+fmt(x,4)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Y</span><b>'+fmt(y,4)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Z</span><b>'+fmt(z,4)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Azimuth واقعی</span><b>'+fmt(a,4)+'°</b></div><button type="button" class="btn primary" style="margin-top:8px" onclick="saveCalculatedPoint();return false;">✓ SAVE POINT</button></div>';return false;}
function saveCalculatedPoint(){if(!lastCalculatedPoint){showToast('Calculate point first');return false;}points.push(lastCalculatedPoint);saveJSON(POINTS_KEY,points);lastCalculatedPoint=null;prepareNewPoint();updateAllUI();showToast('Point saved ✓');return false;}
function refreshPoints(){var list=$("pointsList");if(!list)return;$("pointsJobName").textContent=job.name||'-';if(!points.length){list.innerHTML='<div class="empty">No points stored.</div>';return;}var out='',i,p;for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];out+='<div class="point"><div class="point-head"><b>'+escapeHTML(p.no)+'</b><span>DP: '+escapeHTML(p.dp||'-')+'</span></div><div class="point-body"><div class="coord"><div><span>X</span><b>'+fmt(p.x,4)+'</b></div><div><span>Y</span><b>'+fmt(p.y,4)+'</b></div><div><span>Z</span><b>'+fmt(p.z,4)+'</b></div></div><div class="point-actions"><button type="button" class="small-btn" onclick="editPoint('+p.id+');return false;">EDIT</button><button type="button" class="small-btn delete" onclick="deletePoint('+p.id+');return false;">DELETE</button></div></div></div>';}list.innerHTML=out;}
function deletePoint(id){var a=[];for(var i=0;i<points.length;i++)if(points[i].id!==id)a.push(points[i]);points=a;saveJSON(POINTS_KEY,points);refreshPoints();updateAllUI();showToast('Point deleted');}
function clearAllPoints(){if(!points.length){v5ImportedFileKeys={};try{sessionStorage.removeItem('miladgeo_v5_imported_files_'+JOB_KEY);}catch(e){}var fi=$("v5PointTextFile");if(fi)fi.value='';try{if(window.miladJobAutoSync362)window.miladJobAutoSync362(POINTS_KEY);}catch(e){}showToast('Point list is empty — import lock reset');return;}if(window.confirm&&!window.confirm('CLEAR ALL POINTS?'))return;points=[];v5ImportedFileKeys={};try{localStorage.removeItem(POINTS_KEY);sessionStorage.removeItem('miladgeo_v5_imported_files_'+JOB_KEY);}catch(e){}try{if(window.miladJobAutoSync362)window.miladJobAutoSync362(POINTS_KEY);}catch(e){}var fi=$("v5PointTextFile");if(fi)fi.value='';refreshPoints();updateAllUI();showToast('ALL POINTS CLEARED ✓ — file can be imported again');}
function editPoint(id){var p=null,i;for(i=0;i<points.length;i++)if(points[i].id===id)p=points[i];if(!p)return;$("editId").value=p.id;$("editNo").value=p.no||'';$("editDP").value=p.dp||'';$("editX").value=p.x;$("editY").value=p.y;$("editZ").value=p.z;showPage('edit');}
function updatePoint(){var id=num($("editId").value),p=null,i;for(i=0;i<points.length;i++)if(points[i].id===id)p=points[i];if(!p)return;var requested=$("editNo").value.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'')||p.no;for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){if(points[i].id!==id&&String(points[i].no).toUpperCase()===requested.toUpperCase()){showToast('Point number already exists');return;}}var x=num($("editX").value),y=num($("editY").value),z=num($("editZ").value);if(!finite(x)||!finite(y)||!finite(z)){showToast('Invalid coordinates');return;}p.no=requested;p.dp=$("editDP").value.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'');p.x=x;p.y=y;p.z=z;saveJSON(POINTS_KEY,points);refreshPoints();updateAllUI();showToast('Point updated ✓');showPage('points');}
function prepareManualPoint(){var n=getNextPointNumber();if($("manualPENZDNo"))$("manualPENZDNo").value='P'+('000'+n).slice(-3);if($("manualPENZDX"))$("manualPENZDX").value='';if($("manualPENZDY"))$("manualPENZDY").value='';if($("manualPENZDZ"))$("manualPENZDZ").value='';if($("manualPENZDDP"))$("manualPENZDDP").value='';}
function parsePENZDFields(){var no=String($("manualPENZDNo")?$("manualPENZDNo").value:'').trim()||('P'+String(getNextPointNumber()).padStart(3,'0')),x=num($("manualPENZDX")?$("manualPENZDX").value:''),y=num($("manualPENZDY")?$("manualPENZDY").value:''),z=num($("manualPENZDZ")?$("manualPENZDZ").value:''),dp=String($("manualPENZDDP")?$("manualPENZDDP").value:'').trim();if(!finite(x)||!finite(y)||!finite(z)){showToast('Enter Point, X, Y and Z');return null;}return {no:no,x:x,y:y,z:z,dp:dp};}
function addPENZDPoint(){var p=parsePENZDFields();if(!p)return false;var noKey=String(p.no||'').trim().toUpperCase(),coordKey=[Number(p.x).toFixed(6),Number(p.y).toFixed(6),Number(p.z).toFixed(6)].join('|');for(var i=0;i<points.length;i++){var ep=points[i],epNo=String(ep.no||'').trim().toUpperCase(),epCoord=[Number(ep.x).toFixed(6),Number(ep.y).toFixed(6),Number(ep.z).toFixed(6)].join('|');if(epNo===noKey){showToast(p.no+' already exists in Point List ✓');return false;}if(epCoord===coordKey){showToast('Same coordinates already exist in Point List ✓');return false;}}points.push({id:Date.now()+Math.floor(Math.random()*1000),no:p.no,dp:p.dp,x:p.x,y:p.y,z:p.z,distance:null,azimuth:null,manual:true,penzd:true});saveJSON(POINTS_KEY,points);refreshPoints();updateAllUI();prepareManualPoint();if($('penzdInputStatus'))$('penzdInputStatus').textContent=p.no+' added to Point List ✓';showToast(p.no+' added ✓');return false;}
function clearPENZDInput(){prepareManualPoint();if($('penzdInputStatus'))$('penzdInputStatus').textContent='Point / Easting / Northing / Z / Description';return false;}
function addManualPoint(){return addPENZDPoint();}

function updateStakeSelect(){var el=$("stakeSelect");if(!el)return;var out='<option value="">-- Select Point --</option>',i,p;for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];out+='<option value="'+escapeHTML(String(p.id))+'">'+escapeHTML(p.no)+' — '+escapeHTML(p.dp||'DP')+'</option>';}el.innerHTML=out;}
function loadStakePoint(){var raw=$("stakeSelect")?$("stakeSelect").value:'',p=null,i;for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){if(String(points[i].id)===String(raw)){p=points[i];break;}}$("stakeTarget").innerHTML=p?'<div class="result"><div class="result-line"><span>POINT</span><b>'+escapeHTML(p.no)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>X</span><b>'+fmt(p.x,4)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Y</span><b>'+fmt(p.y,4)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Z</span><b>'+fmt(p.z,4)+'</b></div></div>':'';}
function setStakeMode(mode){
 var a=$("stakePointMode"),b=$("stakePolarMode"),p=$("stakeModePoint"),q=$("stakeModePolar");
 if(!a||!b)return false;
 if(mode==='polar'){a.style.display='none';b.style.display='block';if(p){p.className='btn gray';}if(q){q.className='btn primary';}}
 else {a.style.display='block';b.style.display='none';if(p){p.className='btn primary';}if(q){q.className='btn gray';}}
 return false;
}
function calculateStakePolar(){
 var a=num($("stakeAzInput").value),d=num($("stakeDistInput").value),dh=num($("stakeDzInput").value)||0;
 if(!finite(a)||a<0||a>=360){showToast('Enter Azimuth 0–360');return false;}
 if(!finite(d)||d<0){showToast('Enter Distance');return false;}
 var r=a*Math.PI/180,x=Number(setup.sx)+d*Math.sin(r),y=Number(setup.sy)+d*Math.cos(r),z=Number(setup.sz)+dh;
 $("stakeResult").innerHTML='<div class="result"><div class="az">TARGET COORDINATES</div><div class="result-line"><span>X</span><b>'+fmt(x,4)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Y</span><b>'+fmt(y,4)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Z</span><b>'+fmt(z,4)+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Azimuth</span><b>'+fmt(normalizeAzimuth(a),4)+'°</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Distance</span><b>'+fmt(d,4)+' m</b></div></div>';
 return false;
}
function calculateStake(){
 var id=num($("stakeSelect").value),p=null,i;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++)if(points[i].id===id)p=points[i];
 if(!p){showToast('Select target point');return false;}
 if(!setupComplete()){showToast('Setup is not complete');return false;}
 var dx=p.x-setup.sx,dy=p.y-setup.sy,d=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy),dz=p.z-setup.sz;
 var a=calculateAzimuth(setup.sx,setup.sy,p.x,p.y),turn=normalizeAzimuth(a-setup.azimuth),dir='RIGHT';
 if(turn>180){turn=360-turn;dir='LEFT';}
 var localAngle=(a-setup.azimuth)*Math.PI/180;
 var forward=dx*Math.sin(setup.azimuth*Math.PI/180)+dy*Math.cos(setup.azimuth*Math.PI/180);
 var right=dx*Math.cos(setup.azimuth*Math.PI/180)-dy*Math.sin(setup.azimuth*Math.PI/180);
 var grade=d?dz/d*100:0,slope=Math.sqrt(d*d+dz*dz);
 var tolH=num($("stakeTolH")&&$("stakeTolH").value)||0.02,tolV=num($("stakeTolV")&&$("stakeTolV").value)||0.03;
 var hState=d<=tolH?'WITHIN H TOLERANCE':'MOVE '+fmt(Math.max(0,d-tolH),3)+' m';
 var vState=Math.abs(dz)<=tolV?'WITHIN V TOLERANCE':(dz>0?'LOWER '+fmt(Math.abs(dz)-tolV,3)+' m':'RAISE '+fmt(Math.abs(dz)-tolV,3)+' m');
 $("stakeResult").innerHTML='<div class="result"><div class="az">'+fmt(a,4)+'°</div><div class="result-line"><span>Horizontal Distance</span><b>'+fmt(d,4)+' m</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Slope Distance</span><b>'+fmt(slope,4)+' m</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Turn</span><b>'+dir+' '+fmt(turn,4)+'°</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Forward / Back</span><b>'+fmt(forward,4)+' m</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Right / Left</span><b>'+fmt(right,4)+' m</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>ΔX</span><b>'+fmt(dx,4)+' m</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>ΔY</span><b>'+fmt(dy,4)+' m</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>ΔZ</span><b>'+fmt(dz,4)+' m</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>Grade</span><b>'+fmt(grade,3)+' %</b></div></div>';
 $("stakeNavPanel").innerHTML='<div class="result-line"><span>HORIZONTAL</span><b class="'+(d<=tolH?'status-fixed':'status-float')+'">'+hState+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>VERTICAL</span><b class="'+(Math.abs(dz)<=tolV?'status-fixed':'status-float')+'">'+vState+'</b></div><div class="result-line"><span>TARGET</span><b>'+escapeHTML(p.no)+'</b></div>';
 return false;
}
